<a name="q695n0"></a>
### how do i use tracedb?

→ [`traceDB␣quick␣use:␣set␣TRACEDB_STORE␣to␣s3://bucket/prefix␣or␣a␣local␣dir,␣then␣use␣commands␣tracedb␣put,␣locate,␣plan␣(cost,␣no␣fetch)␣and␣get.` 100%](#q695n144)<br>
<a name="q695n144"></a>**traceDB quick use: set TRACEDB_STORE to s3://bucket/prefix or a local dir, then use commands tracedb put, locate, plan (cost, no fetch) and get.** ■ *(end of answer)*<br>

<a name="q696n0"></a>
### how does tracedb limit fetch size?

→ [`traceDB␣'get'␣is␣budgeted:␣the␣default␣cap␣is␣8␣MiB,␣and␣you␣can␣pass␣--head␣4096␣to␣fetch␣only␣the␣first␣bytes;␣'plan'␣shows␣cost␣without␣fetching.` 100%](#q696n148)<br>
<a name="q696n148"></a>**traceDB 'get' is budgeted: the default cap is 8 MiB, and you can pass --head 4096 to fetch only the first bytes; 'plan' shows cost without fetching.** ■ *(end of answer)*<br>

<a name="q697n83"></a>**traceDB pairs with ontolagni: ontolagni says where data is, and traceDB fetches it.** ■ *(end of answer)*<br>

### what is tracedb?

<a name="q699n123"></a>**traceDB is a fast, byte-budgeted, content-addressed store for agent traces and research data, over S3 or a local directory.** ■ *(end of answer)*<br>

### where does tracedb store data?

→ [`traceDB␣stores␣data␣in␣S3␣or␣a␣local␣directory,␣chosen␣with␣the␣TRACEDB_STORE␣environment␣variable.␣See␣DESIGN.md␣for␣details.` 100%](#q700n126)<br>
<a name="q700n126"></a>**traceDB stores data in S3 or a local directory, chosen with the TRACEDB_STORE environment variable. See DESIGN.md for details.** ■ *(end of answer)*<br>
